## November 17, 2026 — RICOR PI stock-clearing override ROOT CAUSE CONFIRMED on MAIN; accounting design choice NEXT

Owner executed previously pinned **read-only** `ccpc_pr33_ricor_native_srb_rule_readonly_20261117.sh` on `development.localhost` and reported `PINNED_MAIN_DEV_SOURCE=PASS`, `EXACT_DRAFT_PAIR_UNPOSTED=PASS`, `INSTALLED_NATIVE_PI_STOCK_CLEARING_RULE=FOUND`, `READ_ONLY_SRB_DIAGNOSTIC=PASS`, `NO_PI_REVALIDATION_OR_POSTING_PERFORMED=PASS`. The exact flags are **Ricor perpetual inventory YES; Item stock YES; PI is_opening No; not fixed asset; no Purchase Receipt, no Purchase Order detail or drop shipment; update_stock NO**. Company native `stock_received_but_not_billed` is `2301 - Stock Received But Not Billed - RMC`. Saved Ricor PI item account is exactly `2301`; intended CCPC controller expense was `5150 - Intercompany Materials Expense - RMC`. Diagnostic outputs `NATIVE_NO_RECEIPT_CLEARING_RULE_APPLIES=YES` and `SAVED_PI_MATCHES_NATIVE_CLEARING=YES`. No writes, no ledger posting, both original documents still Draft.

**Root cause CONFIRMED, not speculation:** Installed ERPNext native `PurchaseInvoice.set_expense_account(for_validate=True)` takes the stock item+perpetual+no qualifying receipt branch and selects the Stock Received But Not Billed liability, overriding the CCPC custom app's originally supplied expense account; `update_stock=0` does not bypass that native rule. Other three exact invoice accounts matched and were valid. This is a **major accounting-posting blocker** for the intended Ricor `5150` direct expense; `2301` is a valid *liability* account but is a different accounting treatment, not a typographical Chart of Accounts defect.

**Next authorized scope: source-reviewed accounting design/recommendation only; choose business flow before modifying anything.** For genuine Ricor receipt into warehouse, design real inventory Receipt then consumption with native clearing and linked intercompany evidence. If the Withdrawal Slip/CCPC Material Issue means Ricor departments **consume** the materials without receipt into Ricor warehouse, prefer a governed **nonstock Ricor intercompany charge representation** linking immutable original CCPC stock item and frozen source to separately mapped `5150` expense, without global Item master changes, fake PR, SQL account overrides, manual ledger posts, or duplicate billing. The existing `Approved` batch with two committed **Draft** invoices needs a separately reviewed/audited correction path; app controller's `request_cancelled_invoice_correction` applies only after original invoices are **fully cancelled**, not this Draft pair. Any record mutation needs new post-Draft local+Synology verified recovery first and explicit approval. Do not call `submit`, change accounts/users/Item flags, reopen/delete existing batch/invoices, merge PR33 or touch PRODUCTION.

**Ask owner one concrete business question:** For this CCPC → Ricor Withdrawal Slip/Material Issue, **are the materials immediately consumed/charged to a Ricor department or entered into Ricor warehouse stock for later issue?** Their answer selects the accounting design. Original source facts indicate a CCPC Material Issue and no Ricor Purchase Receipt but do not by themselves prove where Ricor physically kept the goods.
